## 1) Temporal Window Construction

L<sub>e</sub>t $\mathcal { T } = \mathbb { R }$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>ca</sub>l<sub>en</sub>d<sub>ar</sub> ti<sub>me</sub> <sub>measure</sub>d i<sub>n</sub> d<sub>ays.</sub> F<sub>or</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>p</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>l <sub>� an</sub>d <sub>sensor</sub> $m \in \{ \mathrm { S } 1 , \mathrm { S } 2 \}$ <sub>,</sub> l<sub>e</sub>t it<sub>s</sub> S<sub>en</sub>ti<sub>ne</sub>l ti<sub>me ser</sub>i<sub>es</sub> b<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e se</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f <sub>va</sub>lid <sub>acqu</sub>i<sub>s</sub>iti<sub>ons</sub>

$$
S ^ { m } ( x ) \subset \mathbb { R } ^ { C _ { m } } \times \mathcal { T } ,\tag{1}
$$

All t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>ne</sub>d <sub>segmen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s s</sub>h<sub>are an</sub> id<sub>en</sub>ti<sub>ca</sub>l t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>rec</sub>i<sub>pe, so</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>any</sub> dif<sub>erence</sub> b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> th<sub>em s</sub>t<sub>ems so</sub>l<sub>e</sub>l<sub>y</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e</sub> i<sub>npu</sub>t <sub>ra</sub>th<sub>er</sub> th<sub>an</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng proce</sub>d<sub>ure.</sub> W<sub>e use</sub> th<sub>e</sub> Adam o<sub>p</sub>timizer (learnin<sub>g</sub> rate $1 0 ^ { - 4 }$ <sub>,</sub> <sub>we</sub>i<sub>g</sub>ht d<sub>ecay</sub> $1 0 ^ { - 4 } )$ <sub>w</sub>ith <sub>a</sub> <sub>cos</sub>i<sub>ne-annea</sub>li<sub>ng sc</sub>h<sub>e</sub>d<sub>u</sub>l<sub>e over</sub> 80 <sub>epoc</sub>h<sub>s, an</sub>d <sub>a</sub> b<sub>a</sub>t<sub>c</sub>h <sub>s</sub>i<sub>ze o</sub>f 64 for PASTIS-R (128×128 cro<sub>p</sub>s) and 96 for DEN (256×256 cro<sub>p</sub>s).

The objective is a class-weighted pixel-wise cross-entropy th<sub>a</sub>t i<sub>gnores</sub> <sub>vo</sub>id/<sub>un</sub>l<sub>a</sub>b<sub>e</sub>l<sub>e</sub>d <sub>p</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>l<sub>s,</sub>

$$
\mathcal { L } = - \frac { \displaystyle \sum _ { i \in \Omega } w _ { y _ { i } } \log \frac { \exp ( z _ { i , y _ { i } } ) } { \sum _ { c \in C } \exp ( z _ { i , c } ) } } { \displaystyle \sum _ { i \in \Omega } w _ { y _ { i } } } ,\tag{5}
$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $\Omega$ is the set of labeled <sub>p</sub>ixels, C the set of classes, $y _ { i }$ th<sub>e</sub> <sub>groun</sub>d<sub>-</sub>t<sub>ru</sub>th <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>p</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>l $i , z _ { i , c }$ th<sub>e pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d l<sub>og</sub>it f<sub>or c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub> $^ { c , }$ <sub>an</sub>d th<sub>e</sub> <sub>norma</sub>li<sub>za</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> b<sub>y</sub> $\textstyle \sum _ { i } w _ { y _ { i } }$ f<sub>o</sub>ll<sub>ows</sub> th<sub>e we</sub>i<sub>g</sub>ht<sub>e</sub>d<sub>-mean</sub> <sub>re</sub>d<sub>uc</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>o</sub>f th<sub>e</sub> l<sub>oss.</sub> T<sub>o</sub> <sub>m</sub>iti<sub>ga</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub> i<sub>m</sub>b<sub>a</sub>l<sub>ance,</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>per-c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub> <sub>we</sub>i<sub>g</sub>ht i<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e</sub> i<sub>nverse</sub> <sub>square</sub> <sub>roo</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f th<sub>e</sub> <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub> f<sub>requency,</sub>

$$
\tilde { w } _ { c } = \left\{ \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { p _ { c } } } , \quad N _ { c } > 0 \right. , \qquad w _ { c } = \frac { | { \cal C } ^ { + } | } { \sum _ { c ^ { \prime } } \tilde { w } _ { c ^ { \prime } } } \tilde { w } _ { c } ,\tag{6}
$$

## 3) Evaluation Metrics

All models are scored at the <sub>p</sub>ixel level over the classes C defined b<sub>y</sub> each benchmark (|C| = 8 for LUCAS, |C| = 19 for PASTIS<sub>-</sub>R <sub>an</sub>d $\left| C \right| = 6$ for DEN), usin<sub>g</sub> four com<sub>p</sub>lementar<sub>y</sub> <sub>me</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>cs repor</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>as percen</sub>t<sub>ages: overa</sub>ll <sub>accuracy,</sub> b<sub>a</sub>l<sub>ance</sub>d <sub>accuracy, macro-average</sub>d F1 <sub>score an</sub>d <sub>mean</sub> I<sub>n</sub>t<sub>ersec</sub>ti<sub>on-over-</sub> Union (mIoU), the latter bein<sub>g</sub> the standard <sub>p</sub>rimar<sub>y</sub> metric f<sub>or</sub> <sub>seman</sub>ti<sub>c</sub> <sub>segmen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> A<sub>ppen</sub>di<sub>x</sub> <sub>g</sub>i<sub>ves</sub> th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>orma</sub>l d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>n</sub>iti<sub>ons o</sub>f th<sub>e me</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>cs an</sub>d th<sub>e averag</sub>i<sub>ng conven</sub>ti<sub>ons.</sub>

$$

## 1) Overall Accuracy

Th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>rac</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>correc</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub>ifi<sub>e</sub>d <sub>p</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>l<sub>s,</sub>

$$
\mathsf { A c c } = \frac { \sum _ { c \in C } \mathrm { T P } _ { c } } { \sum _ { c \in C } \mathrm { \left( T P _ { { { c } } } + F N _ { { { c } } } \right) } } ,\tag{9}
$$

<sub>w</sub>hi<sub>c</sub>h <sub>equa</sub>l<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>o</sub>t<sub>a</sub>l <sub>num</sub>b<sub>er o</sub>f <sub>correc</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> l<sub>a</sub>b<sub>e</sub>l<sub>e</sub>d <sub>p</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>l<sub>s</sub> di<sub>v</sub>id<sub>e</sub>d b<sub>y</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>o</sub>t<sub>a</sub>l <sub>num</sub>b<sub>er</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>p</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>l<sub>s.</sub> O<sub>vera</sub>ll <sub>accuracy</sub> i<sub>s</sub> d<sub>om</sub>i<sub>na</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d b<sub>y</sub> f<sub>requen</sub>t <sub>c</sub>l<sub>asses</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>can</sub> th<sub>ere</sub>f<sub>ore</sub> b<sub>e</sub> <sub>m</sub>i<sub>s</sub>l<sub>ea</sub>di<sub>ng</sub> <sub>un</sub>d<sub>er</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>rong</sub> <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub> i<sub>m</sub>b<sub>a</sub>l<sub>ance</sub> t<sub>yp</sub>i<sub>ca</sub>l <sub>o</sub>f l<sub>an</sub>d<sub>-cover</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>crop-</sub>t<sub>ype</sub> <sub>maps.</sub>

## 2) Balanced Accuracy

To com<sub>p</sub>ensate for class imbalance, <sub>p</sub>er-class recall (true <sub>p</sub>ositive rate) is avera<sub>g</sub>ed as,

$$
\begin{array} { c } { \displaystyle \mathrm { R e c a l l } _ { c } = \frac { \mathrm { T P } _ { c } } { \mathrm { T P } _ { c } + \mathrm { F N } _ { c } } , } \\ { \displaystyle \mathrm { B a l A c c } = \frac { 1 } { | C | } \sum _ { c \in C } \mathrm { R e c a l l } _ { c } , } \end{array}\tag{10}
$$

<sub>so</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>eac</sub>h <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub> <sub>con</sub>t<sub>r</sub>ib<sub>u</sub>t<sub>es</sub> <sub>equa</sub>ll<sub>y</sub> <sub>regar</sub>dl<sub>ess</sub> <sub>o</sub>f it<sub>s</sub> <sub>p</sub>i<sub>xe</sub>l <sup>fre</sup>qu<sup>enc</sup>y.

## 3) Macro F1 Score

F<sub>or eac</sub>h <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass, prec</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>on an</sub>d <sub>reca</sub>ll <sub>are compu</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d<sub>, an</sub>d th<sub>e</sub>i<sub>r</sub> h<sub>armon</sub>i<sub>c mean</sub> i<sub>s cons</sub>id<sub>ere</sub>d t<sub>o o</sub>bt<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub> th<sub>e per-c</sub>l<sub>ass</sub> F1 <sub>score,</sub>

$$
\begin{array} { c } { \mathrm { P r e c i s i o n } _ { c } = \displaystyle \frac { \mathrm { T P } _ { c } } { \mathrm { T P } _ { c } + \mathrm { F P } _ { c } } , } \\ { \mathrm { F 1 } _ { c } = \displaystyle \frac { 2 \mathrm { P r e c i s i o n } _ { c } \cdot \mathrm { R e c a l l } _ { c } } { \mathrm { P r e c i s i o n } _ { c } + \mathrm { R e c a l l } _ { c } } . } \end{array}\tag{11}
$$

Th<sub>e macro</sub> F1 <sub>score</sub> i<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e unwe</sub>i<sub>g</sub>ht<sub>e</sub>d <sub>mean over c</sub>l<sub>asses,</sub>

$$
\mathrm { F } 1 _ { \mathrm { m a c r o } } = { \frac { 1 } { | C | } } \sum _ { c \in C } \mathrm { F } 1 _ { c } .\tag{12}
$$

Lik<sub>e</sub> b<sub>a</sub>l<sub>ance</sub>d <sub>accuracy,</sub> th<sub>e macro average</sub> t<sub>rea</sub>t<sub>s a</sub>ll <sub>c</sub>l<sub>asses</sub> <sub>equa</sub>ll<sub>y,</sub> b<sub>u</sub>t b<sub>y</sub> <sub>com</sub>bi<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>prec</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>on</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>reca</sub>ll it <sub>a</sub>dditi<sub>ona</sub>ll<sub>y</sub> penalizes over-prediction of majority classes.

Th<sub>e</sub> J<sub>accar</sub>d i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>ex</sub> f<sub>or c</sub>l<sub>ass � measures</sub> th<sub>e over</sub>l<sub>ap</sub> b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> th<sub>e pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>an</sub>d <sub>groun</sub>d<sub>-</sub>t<sub>ru</sub>th <sub>reg</sub>i<sub>ons,</sub>

$$
